New status page `portal/integrations.php` (all signed-in users) with a staleness traffic light where a failed probe beats staleness and a never-seen wire source escalates to warning; dashboard tile; VM-detail client-phase panel; new log category `mecm`; IP allowlist + report-token + probe/threshold management added to `portal/settings.php`.
- New `maintenance-worker` compose service (`Docker/WebAPI/lib/maintenance_worker.php`, mirrors the deploy-worker loop) owning an active TCP MECM reachability probe (target auto-derived from the last device-sync heartbeat, overridable), retention purges (client events, `deploy_logs`, retired catalog rows) — the per-request `removeLog()` in `function.php` was removed — and sparse danger/missing transition audits.
- Data-quality guards (E2): `Validator::netbiosHostname` for `vm_hostname` (max 15, no dots, grandfathered legacy values with a visible warning), global `vm_name` uniqueness across non-template missions plus a template-clone conflict preflight, canonical MAC handling (`lib/mac.php`, migration `0008`) applied at all lookups and writes with a duplicate/ambiguous-NIC import guard (`db_importMAC.php` new `duplicate_macs` field), and a repo-layer mission-rename lock once VMs are submitted/registered in MECM.
- Package sync hardening (E3, `mecm_packages.php`): catalog entries missing from a payload are retired instead of deleted (the old DELETE cascaded into `deploy_vm_packages`), a threshold brake rejects mass-retire payloads with 409, names are split into `package_basename`/`package_version` (migration `0009`), and assignments relink from a retired version to its active successor; unreferenced retired rows are purged after 30 days. Portal package list gains a status filter and the VM picker hides retired entries except already-linked ones with update-available hints.
- Versioned, sanitized PowerShell under `Powershell-MECM/`: server scripts (`mecm/`) and client phase scripts (`clients/`) now read all environment specifics from `HKLM:\SOFTWARE\VirtuSphere\MECM`/`…\VirtuSphere` (no hardcoded IPs/DNS/UNC/site codes), send heartbeats/phase reports, and fix the autoimporter wildcard-delete and `LogonRequirementType` enum; a new idempotent installer registers the three scheduled tasks with no runtime limit. Removed dead repo functions and the legacy root scripts; ADR-0019 records the deferred E3 wire-retirement candidates.

## 2026-07-07

- Replaced the bare-text `400` "Ungültiges CSRF-Token." dead-end on the standalone login page (`portal/login.php`) with a localized `flash_set()` + `redirect_to('login.php')`, matching the 404 flash pattern used elsewhere. An expired/stale login form now redirects back to a working form (fresh session cookie + token) showing the new `login.session_expired` message ("Ihre Sitzung ist abgelaufen. Bitte melden Sie sich erneut an." / "Your session expired. Please log in again.") instead of a layout-less error string; the login page now also renders `flash_messages()`. Token verification itself is unchanged.
- Portal design/accessibility pass driven by a full light/dark, desktop/mobile screenshot review. Fixed a mobile horizontal-overflow bug where wide data tables pushed every page ~2x wider than the viewport: grid/stack children now carry `min-width: 0` (`portal/assets/css/components.css`) so tables scroll inside `.table-wrap` instead of the page. Filled-button colors moved to per-theme `--btn-bg`/`--btn-fg` tokens (`base.css`, applied in `components.css`) so `.button`/`.button-danger` keep WCAG-AA contrast in dark mode (previously white text on the brightened dark-theme accent/danger fills). Added a global `:focus-visible` ring and switched the base font to a local system-font stack. Below 860px the sidebar nav now collapses behind a header toggle (`[data-nav-toggle]` in `lib/layout.php`, handler in `assets/app.js`, styles in `layout.css`, new `layout.nav_toggle` DE/EN key) so content is reachable without scrolling past the whole nav. Smaller cleanups: dashboard Missions/Templates KPI cards are now links, log/user timestamp cells use a `.nowrap` utility, the redundant per-row field labels in the OS/VLAN inline-edit tables are `sr-only`, and the topbar logout button is now `.button-ghost` so per-page primary actions dominate. Documented the durable rules in ADR-0013 and AGENTS.md.
- Replaced the bare-text 404 responses on `portal/vms.php`, `portal/mission_details.php`, `portal/vm_edit.php` and the HTML path of `portal/deploy_log.php` (which rendered a layout-less white page) with a localized `flash_set()` + `redirect_to()` to the parent list; the JSON path of `deploy_log.php` keeps its `http_response_code(404)` + JSON envelope. Mapped the English `ansible_resolve_api_base_url()` RuntimeException to the existing `settings.api_base_url_missing` catalog key in `portal_error_message()` so the deploy page no longer leaks a raw English string, and routed the deploy POST catch through `portal_error_message()`. Fixed remaining ASCII-transliterated umlauts in the `settings` DE catalog (`spaeter`/`fuer`/`Rueckmeldungen`/`Massgeblich`/`gueltige`).
- Hardened repo hygiene and auth after the SSoT/security audit (`docs/audits/2026-07-07-ssot-security-audit.md`): untracked `bin/`/`obj/`/`VirtuSphere_TemporaryKey.pfx` (removing stale divergent `bin/Debug/Ansible` playbook copies), replaced raw exception messages in the legacy token API 500 path with server-side logging plus a generic message, and equalized login timing for unknown usernames with a dummy bcrypt comparison. De-changelogged the README and deduplicated AGENTS.md against GROK.md §1.
- Adopted the LagerPro drift-check/ops patterns: `scripts/check-enum-sync.sh` (PHP-Const-SSoT vs. ENUM mirrors in `struktur.sql`/`migrate.php`), `scripts/check-php-version-sync.sh` (Dockerfile `FROM` as SSoT), `scripts/check-doc-hygiene.sh` (changelog-marker ban + line budgets for always-loaded agent docs), all wired quietly into the SessionStart hook; new PostToolUse hooks `php-lint.sh` (blocking container/host `php -l`) and `lang-parity.sh`; `scripts/backup.sh` + `scripts/restore_test.sh` with `docs/operations/backup.md` runbook (restore proof against a throwaway MySQL container); and `PRE-SHIP-CHECKLIST.md` for release gates.

- Changed the topbar account avatar (`.account-avatar` in `portal/assets/css/layout.css`) from a solid accent-filled circle to an outlined one (accent border, `--accent-strong` text), matching the existing secondary-button and active-nav treatment. Solid accent fill was being reused for both "primary action" (buttons) and "current identity" (avatar), which is ambiguous; fill is now reserved exclusively for primary/destructive actions. Documented the rule in ADR-0013.
- Switched `.button-secondary`/`.button-ghost` (`portal/assets/css/components.css`) and the topbar `.account-link`/`.account-avatar` (`portal/assets/css/layout.css`) from a `--surface` background to `--surface-muted`, since `--surface` is also the background of the panels/cards these controls sit on and made the outline the only visible cue that they were interactive controls at all.
- Introduced `portal_format_timestamp()` in `lib/layout.php` as the SSoT for display-only date/time rendering (`d.m.Y H:i:s`) and routed every raw MySQL timestamp echo through it: log viewer (`portal/logs.php`), deploy jobs and their log stream (`portal/deploy.php`, `portal/deploy_log.php`, including the JSON polling endpoint consumed by `assets/app.js`), missions list/dashboard (`portal/missions.php`, `portal/dashboard.php`), credentials (`portal/credentials.php`), packages (`portal/packages.php`) and users' locked-until/last-seen columns (`portal/users.php`). The hidden `updated_at` optimistic-concurrency fields on `portal/vm_edit.php` and `portal/mission_details.php` were left as raw ISO strings since they are compared byte-for-byte against the DB column, not displayed. Documented the convention in `GROK.md` and `AGENTS.md`.
- Added pagination (50 rows/page, prev/next nav) and a category filter/column to the log viewer (`portal/logs.php`). New `deploy_logs.category` column (migration `0006_log_category`, indexed, defaults existing rows to `system`) is populated by every `audit()`/`addLog()` call site via the new `VIRTUSPHERE_LOG_CATEGORY_*` constants in `lib/constants.php` (`users`, `credentials`, `missions`, `os`, `settings`, `vms`, `vlans`, `deploy`, `legacy_api`, `system`), so entries can be filtered/grouped by the module that wrote them instead of only by free-text search or IP.
- Added an in-app "Help" page (`portal/help.php`, nav entry for all authenticated roles) with a project how-to (mission/template/deploy workflow), an Administrator-vs-User capability comparison, the five VM status stages and the deploy modes; explained the `Ansible -vvv` deploy checkbox inline with a hint instead of the bare flag name. New `lang/{de,en}/help.php` catalog module.
- Cleaned up the `portal/users.php` action column, which previously let the deactivate button, role dropdown and password reset controls wrap unevenly at mismatched widths; each control now stacks in a consistent full-width row. Active/must-change-password now render as badges instead of raw `1`/`0`, and empty locked-until/last-seen cells show a muted dash instead of blank space. Also corrected leftover ASCII-transliterated umlauts in the `users` DE catalog (`Zuruecksetzen` -> `Zurücksetzen`, etc.).
- Closed the Phase C hardening verification slice: portal login now maps the `ip_locked` auth reason to the dedicated translated lockout message, and new static regression coverage locks down debug-gated error pages, generic machine-API failures, type-scoped MECM package sync, portal permission/admin guards, validator i18n, generic health failures, MAC lookup migration, deploy-worker heartbeat and stale-job reaper contracts.

- Added an integration regression test for deploy-job reaping and terminal-job locking. The test creates isolated `phpunit_phase_c_*` missions, skips instead of mutating unrelated running jobs, proves stale running jobs are marked failed with a SYSTEM log entry, and proves `repo_finish_deploy_job()` only succeeds for the worker that still owns a running lock.

- Started and completed the Phase D dead-code cleanup: removed retired 410 tombstones, old portal passthrough shims and `portal/vorgaben.txt`, switched the standalone login page to the existing versioned CSS assets, and untracked ignored runtime logs under `Docker/WebAPI/api/`.
- Replaced the raw `Lebenszyklus: ready, MECM: not_ready, updated=0, mecm_id=...` diagnostic string on `portal/vm_edit.php` with colored lifecycle/MECM-sync badges (new `lifecycle_badge()`/`mecm_sync_badge()` helpers in `lib/layout.php`, backed by `virtusphere_lifecycle_meta()`/`virtusphere_mecm_sync_meta()` in `lib/status.php`) plus a dedicated read-only MECM-ID field with a "None"/"Keine" fallback instead of a blank/raw value. Applied the same MECM-sync badge to the `portal/vms.php` list column, which had the identical raw-text pattern.

## 2026-07-06

- Added the first QA/hook baseline: blocking hard-pattern hook via `scripts/lint-csp-patterns.sh`, PHPUnit config/tests for language catalog, permission parity and machine API wire checks, nginx deny for `/tests/`, and ADR-0015 documenting the no-PHPStan/no-CI scope for this stage.

- Hardened security and deploy edge cases: generic browser error pages unless `VIRTUSPHERE_DEBUG=1`, generic machine-API 500 envelopes with server-side logging, no cross-catalog wipe on partial MECM package sync payloads, `vms.write` protection for MECM reset, last-active-admin guard, login IP-lock messaging/sticky username, generic health failure response, interface MAC lookup index, and deploy-worker heartbeat/reaper handling for stale running jobs.

- Added granular per-VM deploy targeting and a MAC-generation power-cycle mode. The deploy page now lists a mission's VMs as checkboxes (empty selection = whole mission, preserving prior behavior) with a `MAC present/missing` badge, and the job payload carries `vm_ids` (validated against the mission) plus a configurable `powercycle_wait` (default 5s, 1???300s). A new `Power-Cycle + Export MACs` mode (`powercycle`) briefly powers on ??? waits ??? hard powers off only VMs whose WDS-VLAN NIC has no MAC yet (`needs_mac`), then exports MACs; `full` now runs `create ??? power-cycle ??? export ??? start`. New playbook `Ansible/powercycleVMs-ESXi_playbook.yml`. No VM firmware/boot-order change: PXE only completes on the WDS-VLAN NIC (the only one with a PXE responder), so the exported MAC matches the NIC that actually boots. Operating requirement: only the WDS VLAN may provide a PXE/DHCP responder.

- Moved the signed-in user identity out of page content and into the shared portal topbar as a compact account link, removing the always-visible `Angemeldet als ...` strip from portal pages.

- Hardened portal logout edge-case handling: stale logout POSTs without an active session now clear any leftover cookie state and redirect to `login.php`, while GET logout attempts and active-session CSRF mismatches remain HTTP 400.

- Added VM creation defaults and recovery controls: new VMs default to 2 CPU / 4096 MB RAM and a 40 GB `thick` System disk, Guest OS selection now uses the fixed VMware Guest ID SSoT, and VMs can be explicitly requeued for MECM with `Reset MECM ID` when an external MECM device record was deleted or became stale.

- Rejected whitespace in mission and template names. Enforced server-side in `repo_validate_mission_values()` (covers create, edit, template-to-mission clone and mission-to-template capture) with a `pattern="\S+"` guard on the portal name inputs. The rule also applies when editing existing entries, so legacy names containing spaces must be corrected before they can be saved again.

- Updated the ESXi VM-creation playbook (`Ansible/createVMs-ESXi_playbook.yml`) hardware profile for newly deployed VMs: hardware version 21, `hotadd_memory`, and `vpmc_enabled` in addition to the existing `scsi`/`secure_boot`/`efi` settings. Only the create playbook carries a `hardware:` block; `startVMs-` (power-on) is unaffected. The generated `esxi_port` contract and the desktop `bin/` playbook copies are unchanged.

- Hardened deploy-worker runtime resilience: added `restart: unless-stopped` to all compose services and made the CLI worker survive MySQL outages by reconnecting with backoff in `--loop` mode (`db(true)` reconnect helper) instead of crashing and leaving deploy jobs stuck in `queued`. The `--once` tooling mode still fails fast.

## 2026-07-05

- Audited backend/Ops plan completion against the Findings/SSoT update, removed legacy real log files from Git tracking, removed the unused raw machine-API query helper, and documented the remaining future-only boundaries.

- Consolidated the backend SSoT/security cleanup: retired unsafe legacy web entry points with HTTP 410, blocked direct web access to internal runtime paths, centralized credential/deploy/status/role/machine-API literals, and normalized ESXi deploy endpoints for Ansible artifacts.

- Added the portal frontend QOL slice: session-backed sticky forms with per-field validation errors (`lib/forms.php`) wired into the OS/VLAN/package/credential/user/mission/settings forms, per-field and aggregated validation errors on the VM editor, log viewer search/IP filters, and the deploy API base URL settings page.

- Completed the backend-only validation follow-up: credential host/port/duplicate checks, mission/catalog duplicate and missing-ID validation, and deploy-queue preflight for user, mission fields, VM presence and credential completeness.

- Documented the backend-only handoff boundary, current install status and backend/Ops release checklist.

- Added backend validation and ops preflight hardening: shared Validator/ValidationException, VM RAM/CPU/disk/interface checks, IP-wide login throttling, extended JSON health checks, `migrate.php --check`, Ubuntu-friendly setup/env docs, and retired the unsafe root testdata bootstrap.

- Continued Phase 1 backend hardening: checked Ansible artifact writes, made deploy-job terminal updates explicit, removed the silent ESXi probe suppression, and validated migration/MECM result sets.
- Added Phase 1 loud-error infrastructure: global web/CLI error handlers with reference IDs, persistent PHP engine logs, persistent nginx logs, shared log-file boot checks, and PHP 8.4-safe DB connection reuse.
- Hardened the WP1 machine API hotfix slice: retired open self-registration endpoints with HTTP 410, rejected empty MECM package sync payloads before catalog cleanup, and made MAC imports require `mission_id` with explicit unmatched-interface error responses.

## 2026-06-29

- Added deploy runtime settings and Ubuntu Ansible host preflight checks for SSH credentials and worker execution.
- Added the WP5 worker foundation: server-side Ansible artifact generation, SFTP upload, remote playbook execution, queue claiming and deploy log streaming from a separate CLI worker container.

## 2026-06-28

- Started E0 migration baseline: env-based Docker configuration, PHP 8.4 target, shared PHP lib foundation, schema baseline, health endpoint and initial ADR/rules documentation.
- Hardened the legacy token and machine integration surfaces for E1: repository split under `Docker/WebAPI/lib/repo/`, prepared-statement CRUD, transactional VM batch writes, status-event tracking, MAC import by mission payload and MECM package sync fixes.
- Added the E1 portal auth foundation: session login/logout, CSRF, RBAC helpers, user administration, own-password change and first-admin seed support.
- Added the first E2 portal UI slice: shared layout, local CSS/JS assets, dashboard, missions/templates, mission details with template clone, VM list/edit, OS/VLAN/package catalog pages and admin log viewer.
- Added the WP4 credential foundation: libsodium-at-rest credential encryption, admin credential CRUD, short-lived ESXi/SSH connection probes and no plaintext secret display.
- Added the first WP5 deploy queue slice: RBAC-gated deploy job creation, one active job per mission, credential-id snapshots, cancellable queue rows and polling deploy log view.
